        assert content.count("<!-- turn-") == 1, content
        assert content.index(formal_message("发起", STEER_INPUT)) < content.index(formal_message("补充", STEER_ADDITION))
        assert content.index(formal_message("补充", STEER_ADDITION)) < content.index(formal_message("回复", STEER_REPLY))
        assert content.count("\n---\n\n") == 2
        return {"sentDuringActualTool": True, "oneNativeTurn": True, "supplementBeforeFinalInRecord": True}

    try:
        assert not discovered()
        assert "projects" not in tomllib.loads(config.read_text())
        start()
        wait(lambda: "Trust and continue" in screen(), "目录信任提示")
        time.sleep(0.3)
        shell("send-keys", "-t", session, "Enter")
        wait(lambda: "Trust all and continue" in screen(), "Hook 信任提示")
        before_hooks = discovered()
        expected_events = {"userPromptSubmit", "stop", "postToolUse", "preToolUse"} if formal else {"userPromptSubmit", "stop"}
        actual_events = {hook["eventName"] for hook in before_hooks}
        assert {"userPromptSubmit", "stop"} <= actual_events <= expected_events
        assert all(hook["trustStatus"] == "untrusted" for hook in before_hooks)
        shell("send-keys", "-t", session, "Down")
        wait(lambda: "› 2. Trust all" in screen(), "选中 Hook 信任")
        shell("send-keys", "-t", session, "Enter")
        wait(lambda: "gpt-5.4" in screen(), "信任后空白界面")
        hook_details = discovered()
        assert len(hook_details) == len(actual_events) and all(hook["trustStatus"] == "trusted" and hook["enabled"] and
                                              Path(hook["sourcePath"]) == hook_file for hook in hook_details)
        assert {hook["currentHash"] for hook in before_hooks} == {hook["currentHash"] for hook in hook_details}
        skill_discovery = discovered_skill()
        end()
        assert not trace(events) and not (repo / "records").exists()
        start()
        wait(lambda: "gpt-5.4" in screen(), "普通 codex 启动")
        reply_writes = []
        for number, (prompt, answer) in enumerate(zip(PROMPTS, REPLIES), start=1):
            enter(prompt)
            wait(lambda: answer.splitlines()[-1] in screen(), f"轮次 {number} 显示末段")
            check = (lambda: saved_reply(answer)) if formal else (lambda: len(read_backs()) >= number * 2)
            wait(check, f"轮次 {number} 原话与回复读回")
            if formal:
                reply_writes.append(saved_reply(answer).stat().st_mtime_ns)
        commit_proof = None
        if formal:
            enter(COMMIT_PROMPT)
            wait(lambda: COMMIT_REPLY in screen(), "显示提交结果")
            wait(lambda: any('"type":"task_complete"' in line and COMMIT_REPLY in line
                             for path in (state / "sessions").rglob("*.jsonl")
                             for line in path.read_text().splitlines()), "宿主完成提交结果轮次")
            trace_after_reply = records_snapshot(repo)
            commit_requests = [row for row in trace(events) if row["event"] == "ProviderRequest"
                               and row["correlation"] == "commit" and not row["auxiliary"]]
            outputs_by_count = {len(row["commitOutputs"]): row for row in commit_requests}
            assert set(outputs_by_count) == {0, 1, 2, 3}, commit_requests
            assert outputs_by_count[0]["records"] == outputs_by_count[1]["records"], commit_requests
            assert outputs_by_count[1]["records"] != outputs_by_count[2]["records"], commit_requests
            assert outputs_by_count[2]["records"] == outputs_by_count[3]["records"] == trace_after_reply, commit_requests
            outputs = outputs_by_count[3]["commitOutputs"]
            assert "absent.txt" in str(outputs["t035-failure"]), outputs
            assert "repaired" in str(outputs["t035-retry"]), outputs
            assert not saved_reply(COMMIT_REPLY)
            detail = "\n".join(path.read_text() for path in repo.glob("records/*/details/*/*.md"))
            assert "T035_REPAIRED_README" in detail and "absent.txt" not in detail
            enter(AFTER_PROMPT)
            wait(lambda: saved_reply(AFTER_REPLY), "提交后的普通回复继续保存")
            commit_proof = {"realFailureThenSuccess": True, "repairRecordedBeforeRetry": True,
                            "bothCommitWindowsUnchanged": True, "resultReplyOmitted": True, "laterReplyRecorded": True}
        end()
        observed = trace(events)
        records = list(repo.glob("records/*/*.md"))
        assert len(records) == 1, records
        saved = records[0].read_bytes()
        actual_replies = [row for row in observed if row["event"] == "Stop" and row.get("status") == "received"]
        if formal:
            assert all(saved_reply(answer) for answer in REPLIES)
        else:
            assert [row["original"] for row in actual_replies] == REPLIES, actual_replies
        assert all(row.get("exact") for row in read_backs()), read_backs()
        assert saved.count(b"<!-- turn-") == (4 if formal else 2), saved
        assert all(prompt.encode() in saved for prompt in PROMPTS)
        assert sum(row["event"] == "ProviderBusinessTool" for row in observed) == 1, observed
        assert any(row.get("outputHasReadme") for row in observed), observed
        for name, prompt in zip(("readme", "question"), PROMPTS):
            requests = [row for row in observed if row["event"] == "ProviderRequest" and row["correlation"] == name and not row["auxiliary"]]
            relocated = repo / "conversation-recording" / records[0].relative_to(repo)
            assert records[0].read_bytes() == preserved
            assert RESUMES[-1] in relocated.read_text() and "previousRecords" in relocated.read_text()
            assert all(answer not in relocated.read_text() for answer in RESUMES[:-1])
            recovery = {"openingDate": opening_date, "resumedLocalDate": day, "sameRecordAfterTwoResumes": True,
                        "ignoredOriginalPreserved": True, "relocatedStartsWithNewTurn": True}
        if extended:
            (repo / ".gitignore").unlink()
            extended_proof = {"toolMatrix": verify_matrix(), "foreignResume": verify_foreign_resume(),
                              "interruptedStop": verify_interrupted_stop(), "failedWrite": verify_failed_write(),
                              "cancelAndEarlyClose": verify_user_cancellation_and_early_close(),
                              "partialWrite": verify_partial_write(), "parallelMidnight": verify_parallel_midnight(),
                              "sameTurnSupplement": verify_supplement()}
        print(json.dumps({"version": subprocess.check_output([binary, "--version"], text=True).strip(),
                          "preseededTrust": False, "ordinaryLaunch": True, "emptyRecordCount": 0, "formal": formal,
                          "nativeTrustPrompts": ["project", "hooks"], "skillDiscovery": skill_discovery,
                          "installedSkillHashes": installed, "candidateSha256": sha256(candidate.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
                          "hooks": [{"name": hook["eventName"], "trusted": hook["trustStatus"] == "trusted",
                                     "enabled": hook["enabled"], "currentHash": hook["currentHash"],
                                     "source": ".codex/hooks.json"}
                                    for hook in hook_details],
                          "hostFinal": final_entries, "observed": observed, "recordSha256": sha256(saved).hexdigest(),
                          "commit": commit_proof, "recovery": recovery, "extended": extended_proof,
                          "replySavedAfterHostFinal": len(reply_writes) == len(REPLIES),
                          "recordVisible": bool(subprocess.check_output(
                              ["git", "status", "--short", "--untracked-files=all", "--", "records"], cwd=repo)),
                          "controls": cases}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    finally:
        (work / "last-screen.txt").write_text(screen())
        subprocess.run([*tmux, "kill-server"], capture_output=True)
        server.shutdown()
        server.server_close()



~~~

def pretty(value):
    return value if isinstance(value, str) else json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)


def as_mapping(value):
    if isinstance(value, str):
        try:
            value = json.loads(value)
        except ValueError:
            return {}
    return value if isinstance(value, dict) else {}


def commands_from(value):
    """取得直接调用或 Code Mode 中可核实的字面命令。"""
    arguments = as_mapping(value)
    direct = arguments.get("cmd") or arguments.get("command")
    if isinstance(direct, str):
        yield direct
    elif isinstance(value, str):
        for match in CODE_COMMAND.finditer(value):
            literal = match[2] or match[1]
            if literal.isidentifier():
                available = dict(CODE_ASSIGNMENT.findall(value[:match.start()]))
                literal = available.get(literal, "")
            if literal.startswith("`"):
                if "${" not in literal:
                    yield literal[1:-1]
                continue
            try:
                decoded = literal_eval(literal)
            except (ValueError, SyntaxError):
                continue
            if isinstance(decoded, str):
                yield decoded


def command_segments(command):
    """只解析命令字面量，不执行记录里的任何命令。"""
    if not isinstance(command, str):

    def shell(*args):
        return subprocess.check_output([*tmux, *args], stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)

    def screen():
        try:
            return shell("capture-pane", "-p", "-t", session)
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as error:
            return error.output

    def wait(predicate, label, timeout=15):
        limit = time.monotonic() + timeout
        while time.monotonic() < limit:
            result = predicate()
            if result:
                return result
            time.sleep(0.07)
        raise RuntimeError(f"{label}\n{screen()}\n{trace(events)[-8:]}")

    def enter(value):
        shell("send-keys", "-t", session, "-l", value)
        wait(lambda: value in screen(), "终端已回显输入")
        shell("send-keys", "-t", session, "Enter")

    def start(directory=repo, arguments=(), zone=None):
        command = ("env", f"TZ={zone}", binary) if zone else (binary,)
        shell("new-session", "-d", "-s", session, "-x", "125", "-y", "40", "-c", str(directory),
              *command, *arguments)

    def end():
        enter("/exit")
        wait(lambda: "can't find" in screen() or "no server running" in screen(), "正常退出会话")

    def discovered():
        return asyncio.run(list_hooks(binary, repo, (repo,)))[0]["hooks"]

    def discovered_skill():
        scopes = asyncio.run(discover_project_items(binary, repo, "skills/list", {"cwds": [str(repo)], "forceReload": True}))
        skills = scopes[0]["skills"]
        assert not any(item["name"] == "shadow-profile" for item in skills)
        matching = [item for item in skills if item["name"] == "conversation-recording" and item["enabled"]]
        assert len(matching) == 1 and Path(matching[0]["path"]) == skill / "SKILL.md", matching
        return {"installedProjectSkillDiscovered": True, "retiredEntryCount": 0}

    def read_backs():
        return [event for event in trace(events) if event.get("status") == "read-back"]

    def saved_reply(answer):
        expected = formal_message("回复", answer).encode()
        locations = [*repo.glob("records/*/*.md"), *repo.glob("conversation-recording/records/*/*.md")]
        return next((path for path in locations if expected in path.read_bytes()), None)

    def stop_isolated_daemon():
        result = subprocess.run([binary, "app-server", "daemon", "stop"], cwd=repo, text=True,
                                capture_output=True, timeout=15)
        assert result.returncode == 0, (result.stdout, result.stderr)

    def kill_isolated_daemon():
        """终止本探针 PID 命名空间内的原生宿主，保留未完成回调边界。"""
        metadata = json.loads((state / "app-server-daemon/daemon.pid").read_text())
        process = metadata["pid"]
        descriptor = os.pidfd_open(process)

            argv = (Path("/proc") / str(process) / "cmdline").read_bytes().split(b"\0")
            assert b"app-server" in argv and Path(os.fsdecode(argv[0])).name == "codex", argv
            signal.pidfd_send_signal(descriptor, signal.SIGKILL)
            assert select.select([descriptor], [], [], 10)[0], "隔离宿主未退出"
        finally:

        assert any(row.get("outputHasReadme") for row in observed), observed
        for name, prompt in zip(("readme", "question"), PROMPTS):

            assert requests[0]["promptAlreadySaved"], requests
            if not formal:

                prompt_read_back = next(row for row in read_backs() if row["turn"] == original["turn"] and row["event"] == "UserPromptSubmit")
                finished = next(row for row in observed if row["event"] == "ProviderFinished" and row["correlation"] == name and not row["auxiliary"] and row["isFinal"])
                after = next(row for row in read_backs() if row["turn"] == original["turn"] and row["event"] == "Stop")
                assert prompt_read_back["ns"] < requests[0]["ns"] < finished["ns"] < after["ns"], observed
        session_id = records[0].stem
        rollouts = list((state / "sessions").rglob(f"*{session_id}.jsonl"))
        assert len(rollouts) == 1, rollouts
        final_entries = []
        for line in rollouts[0].read_text().splitlines():
            row = json.loads(line)
            payload = row.get("payload", {})
            if row["type"] == "response_item" and payload.get("phase") == "final_answer":
                text = "".join(item.get("text", "") for item in payload["content"])
                final_entries.append({"hostTime": row["timestamp"], "hostText": text})
        assert [entry["hostText"] for entry in final_entries] == REPLIES + ([COMMIT_REPLY, AFTER_REPLY] if formal else []), final_entries
        for entry, write_ns in zip(final_entries[:len(reply_writes)], reply_writes):
            emitted = int(datetime.fromisoformat(entry["hostTime"]).timestamp() * 1e9)

        stop_isolated_daemon()
        file_snapshot = {path: (path.stat().st_mtime_ns, path.read_bytes()) for path in (repo / "records").rglob("*") if path.is_file()}
        cases = []

~~~text
# Agent Foundry 工程约定

本仓库采用 pnpm 12、Turborepo、ESM与严格TypeScript。apps维护文档站和Python MCP；packages维护两条技能工作线；tooling只提供必要共享配置。跨包引用通过声明的exports和workspace依赖。

注释和文档使用简体中文。编辑JS/TS/Vue时先加载document-changed-symbols并为新增或语义修改声明添加多行JSDoc；前端样式和响应式分别遵守nuxt-ui-styling与vue-reactivity，默认不新增watcher。这三项技能来源在packages的对应目录，已安装副本通过pnpm skills:link生成，不手改副本。

主线packages/skills保留用户手写分工；未经本轮明确请求不接管其未完成构建插件。紧急版保持自身源码、脚本与输出，Linux/Codex/项目级之外的组合不推定支持。eval源码保留不等于正式发布内容；当前主线配置与规格的差异见迁移记录。

初始化使用pnpm install；开发pnpm dev；一般构建pnpm build；严格候选单独pnpm build:candidate，要求相关输入已真实提交；lint和check-types按目标workspace执行。有原测试时按范围运行，不存在根pnpm test。Python依赖用uv及apps/mcp/uv.lock。

任何Markdown新增或修改后执行pnpm exec eslint --fix；历史规格、归档和第三方夹具保持发生时原文，公开派生以迁移清单说明。验证失败、缺前置和未执行分别记录，不通过改规则或删断言求绿。

不复制其他项目的私人画像、会话、凭据或机器配置。目标项目技能沿目标项目自身治理与记录布局；不在这里建立跨仓库个人画像。不得提交secrets、环境变量值或认证文件。

改动前查看git status，不覆盖用户未提交文件。生成物不手改。推送、发布、部署和外部资源变更须有适用明确授权。

OUTPUTS = {"function_call_output", "custom_tool_call_output", "mcp_tool_call_output"}
SECRET_NAME = re.compile(r"password|passwd|secret|token|credential|(?:^|_)key(?:_|$)|authorization", re.I)
STRUCTURAL_PATHS = {"PWD", "OLDPWD", "HOME", "CODEX_HOME", "CODEX_SQLITE_HOME", "PATH", "TMPDIR", "XDG_RUNTIME_DIR"}
SECRET_VALUE = re.compile(r"(?i)(sk-[a-z0-9_-]{12,}|gh[pousr]_[a-z0-9]{15,}|bearer\s+[^\s,;]+|[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*://[^\s/:@]+:[^\s/@]+@)")
ASSIGNMENT = re.compile(r"(?m)(\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*(?:_TOKEN|_KEY|_PASSWORD|_SECRET)\s*=\s*)([^\s,;]+)")
SENSITIVE_PATH = re.compile(r'''(?i)(?:^|[/\s'"])(?:\.env(?:\.[\w.-]+)?|\.netrc|auth\.json|credentials(?:\.json)?|id_rsa|id_ed25519)(?=$|[/\s'"])''')
JS_LITERAL = r'''"(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*"|'(?:[^'\\]|\\.)*'|`[^`]*`'''
CODE_COMMAND = re.compile(rf'''\btools\.(?:exec_command|shell_command)\s*\(\s*\{{[^{{}}]*?\b(cmd|command)\s*(?::\s*({JS_LITERAL}|\w+)|(?=[,}}]))''')
CODE_ASSIGNMENT = re.compile(rf"\bconst\s+(\w+)\s*=\s*({JS_LITERAL})\s*[;\n]")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Attachment:
    name: str
    data: bytes


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Event:
    location: str
    label: str
    value: str
    attachments: tuple[Attachment, ...] = ()
    identity: str | None = None
    provisional: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TurnContents:
    """当轮宿主事件与供工具回调补齐结果的调用身份。"""

    events: tuple[Event, ...]
    inputs: tuple[str, ...]
    commit_result: bool
    tool_privacy: dict[str, bool]
    tool_outputs: frozenset[str]


def redacted(text):
    """保留可记录原文，明确标记宿主账号或密钥值的省略。"""
    values = {value for name, value in os.environ.items() if name not in STRUCTURAL_PATHS
              and value and (len(value) >= 8 or SECRET_NAME.search(name))}
    if values:
        alternatives = "|".join(re.escape(value) for value in sorted(values, key=len, reverse=True))
        text = re.sub(alternatives, "[已省略环境变量值]", text)
    text = SECRET_VALUE.sub("[已省略凭据]", text)
    return ASSIGNMENT.sub(r"\1[已省略环境变量值]", text)



~~~

    "text": "{\"chunk_id\":\"7a3aa9\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000033941,\"exit_code\":1,\"original_token_count\":608,\"output\":\"# Agent Foundry 工程约定\\n\\n本仓库采用 pnpm 12、Turborepo、ESM与严格TypeScript。apps维护文档站和Python MCP；packages维护两条技能工作线；tooling只提供必要共享配置。跨包引用通过声明的exports和workspace依赖。\\n\\n注释和文档使用简体中文。编辑JS/TS/Vue时先加载document-changed-symbols并为新增或语义修改声明添加多行JSDoc；前端样式和响应式分别遵守nuxt-ui-styling与vue-reactivity，默认不新增watcher。这三项技能来源在packages的对应目录，已安装副本通过pnpm skills:link生成，不手改副本。\\n\\n主线packages/skills保留用户手写分工；未经本轮明确请求不接管其未完成构建插件。紧急版保持自身源码、脚本与输出，Linux/Codex/项目级之外的组合不推定支持。eval源码保留不等于正式发布内容；当前主线配置与规格的差异见迁移记录。\\n\\n初始化使用pnpm install；开发pnpm dev；一般构建pnpm build；严格候选单独pnpm build:candidate，要求相关输入已真实提交；lint和check-types按目标workspace执行。有原测试时按范围运行，不存在根pnpm test。Python依赖用uv及apps/mcp/uv.lock。\\n\\n任何Markdown新增或修改后执行pnpm exec eslint --fix；历史规格、归档和第三方夹具保持发生时原文，公开派生以迁移清单说明。验证失败、缺前置和未执行分别记录，不通过改规则或删断言求绿。\\n\\n不复制其他项目的私人画像、会话、凭据或机器配置。目标项目技能沿目标项目自身治理与记录布局；不在这里建立跨仓库个人画像。不得提交secrets、环境变量值或认证文件。\\n\\n改动前查看git status，不覆盖用户未提交文件。生成物不手改。推送、发布、部署和外部资源变更须有适用明确授权。\\npackages/skills/tests/test_conversation_recording.py:293:    def test_code_mode_git_commit_result_is_omitted(self):\\npackages/skills/tests/test_conversation_recording.py:295:        call = {\\\"ordinal\\\": 2, \\\"type\\\": \\\"response_item\\\", \\\"payload\\\": {\\\"type\\\": \\\"custom_tool_call\\\", \\\"name\\\": \\\"exec\\\",\\npackages/skills/tests/test_conversation_recording.py:303:    def test_native_execution_evidence_covers_opaque_code_mode_commit(self):\\npackages/skills/tests/test_conversation_recording.py:305:        code = {\\\"type\\\": \\\"response_item\\\", \\\"payload\\\": {\\\"type\\\": \\\"custom_tool_call\\\", \\\"name\\\": \\\"exec\\\",\\n\"}"
  }
]

    def test_git_ignored_previous_location_stays_unchanged(self):
        self.install()
        self.seed_turn()
        self.callback("UserPromptSubmit", prompt="首轮原话")
        original = self.record.read_bytes()
        (self.repo / ".gitignore").write_text("/records/\n")
        self.turn = str(uuid4())
        self.seed_turn()
        self.callback("UserPromptSubmit", prompt="次轮原话")
        self.callback("Stop", last_assistant_message="次轮回复")
        relocated = self.repo / "conversation-recording" / self.record.relative_to(self.repo)
        self.assertEqual(self.record.read_bytes(), original)
        self.assertIn("previousRecords", relocated.read_text())
        self.assertIn("次轮回复", relocated.read_text())
        self.assertNotIn("首轮原话", relocated.read_text())
        (self.repo / ".gitignore").unlink()
        self.turn = str(uuid4())
        self.seed_turn()
        self.callback("UserPromptSubmit", prompt="继续在迁移后的文件")
        self.assertIn("继续在迁移后的文件", relocated.read_text())
        self.assertEqual(self.record.read_bytes(), original)

    def test_code_mode_git_commit_result_is_omitted(self):
        self.install()
        call = {"ordinal": 2, "type": "response_item", "payload": {"type": "custom_tool_call", "name": "exec",
                "call_id": "code-commit", "input": 'const cmd = "git commit -m literal"; text(await tools.exec_command({cmd}));'}}
        self.seed_turn(events=[call, self.tool_output(3, "code-commit", "[main] literal")])
        self.callback("UserPromptSubmit", prompt="提交")
        saved = self.record.read_bytes()
        self.callback("Stop", last_assistant_message="提交完成")
        self.assertEqual(self.record.read_bytes(), saved)

    def test_native_execution_evidence_covers_opaque_code_mode_commit(self):
        self.install()
        code = {"type": "response_item", "payload": {"type": "custom_tool_call", "name": "exec",
                "call_id": "opaque", "input": "text(await tools.exec_command({cmd: buildCommand()}))"}}
        executed = {"type": "event_msg", "payload": {"type": "item_completed", "turn_id": self.turn,
                    "item": {"type": "CommandExecution", "id": "native", "command": ["[已省略环境变量值]", "-lc", "env -i git commit -m safe"],
                             "aggregated_output": "已提交", "cwd": self.repo.as_uri(), "exit_code": 0}}}
        self.seed_turn(events=[code, executed, self.tool_output(4, "opaque", "提交结果")])
        self.callback("UserPromptSubmit", prompt="完成提交")
        previous = self.record.read_bytes()
        self.callback("Stop", last_assistant_message="提交完成")
        self.assertEqual(self.record.read_bytes(), previous)

    def test_stop_keeps_detail_already_confirmed_by_tool_hook(self):
        self.install()
        self.seed_turn(events=[self.tool_call(2, "during", {"cmd": "cat README.md"})])
        self.callback("UserPromptSubmit", prompt="读一下")
        self.callback("PostToolUse", tool_name="Bash", tool_use_id="during", tool_input={"command": "cat README.md"},
                      tool_response="CALLBACK_ONLY")
        detail = self.record.parent / f"details/{self.session}/{self.turn}.md"
        self.assertIn("CALLBACK_ONLY", detail.read_text())
        self.callback("Stop", last_assistant_message="已读")
        self.assertIn("CALLBACK_ONLY", detail.read_text())

    def test_stop_backfills_attachment_after_callback_result(self):
        self.install()
        arguments = {"cmd": "cat README.md"}
        call = self.tool_call(2, "during", arguments)
        self.seed_turn(events=[call])
        self.callback("UserPromptSubmit", prompt="取附件")
        self.callback("PostToolUse", tool_name="Bash", tool_use_id="during", tool_input=arguments,
                      tool_response="回调先收到文本")
        detail = self.record.parent / f"details/{self.session}/{self.turn}.md"
        self.assertIn("回调先收到文本", detail.read_text())

        original = b"attachment from the host"
        output = self.tool_output(3, "during", [{"type": "image", "name": "report.png",
                                  "mimeType": "image/png", "data": b64encode(original).decode()}])
        self.seed_turn(events=[call, output])
        self.callback("Stop", last_assistant_message="完成")

- **SC-004** 在真实的「修复后提交」样例中，可回查用户请求、首次提交前的修复和后续普通对话。在同一轮提交失败、修复再重试的样例中，还可回查两次提交之间的修复。每次提交步骤及其结果回复期间的记录写入次数为零，且由记录引入的工作区和暂存区新增差异为零。普通轮次的主记录、各组详情和会话附件在 Git 工作区可见，符合提交约定的文件可随项目变更一同提交。
- **SC-005** 使用本仓库至少两个项目目录的已保存材料得到一份协作 Skill。该次生成的每条规则均可经提炼文档回查到原始依据，参与材料的来源均可核实位于本仓库目录内。首次自动提炼时，每条特点的依据均可回查新流程记录。两个父会话从同一版本派发分析后，先采纳的结果推进当前版本，随后返回的旧结果及与删除交错的旧结果均不回退当前规则、依据关联或目录内容。
- **SC-006** 一位使用者将产物目录带到另一项目后，兼容代理可以仅凭该目录在至少一个代表性任务中正确应用其中的规则。
- **SC-007** 删除测试中，一条规则及其独占提炼依据被移除，共享依据及原始记录完整可查。后续符合条件的一次处理能够基于保留的材料重新形成该规则。
- **SC-008** 旧技能的归档内容与原件一致，项目经正常安装或同步后可发现的旧入口数量为零。
- **SC-009** 采用通用 Skill 格式核验时，记录、提炼和分析三份 Skill 各有一个独立、可读的入口及可定位的必要资源，各自的输入、触发条件和产物都能核对。
- **SC-010** 在隔离用例中让父代理启动两个业务子代理但不等待，至少一个子代理在下一用户轮开始后才回复。用不同的已知输入和回复对照同一主记录的顺序、时间和代理标注；主代理回复在子代理结束前已保存并可回查，两份子代理回复按实际时序写入且不混淆身份。无子代理的对照保持原有样式和保存时机。
- **SC-011** 打开实际生成的普通对话主记录，核对同轮及跨轮的每两个相邻消息块之间均可看到分隔；多行消息、消息正文内的分隔符及代码块原样回读，详情链接仍可用。恢复续写后新边界不漏、不重复；主、子代理时间线以 SC-010 的交错样例补验同样的显示规则。

## Assumptions
52:- 用户确认记录 Skill 安装即启用，并明确首期资料只落在项目目录内。用户按日常方式启动 Codex 即进入既有记录流程。会话范围、提交例外和写入失败依 FR-006～FR-008 处理。[用户原话](requirements/raw/2026-11-24-recording-install-enables.md)
197:- **FR-002** 项目级记录安装后，用户通过 Codex 原生流程对本次安装的记录 Hook 确认一次并启用记录。同一项目的 Hook 定义和 Codex 信任状态保持不变时，后续普通启动无需再次确认。用户在本仓库根目录或子目录按日常方式运行 `codex` 并发送消息，无需手改配置、点名记录 Skill 或另行启动记录。每个符合条件的交互轮次都自动进入记录流程，包括模型未选用记录 Skill 且零模型工具调用的轮次。用户发出的第一条正常对话消息触发 FR-003 建档，后续轮次沿同一会话续写。纳入记录的用户发言须在对应业务动作前保存并读回原文。无业务动作时，在开始发送最终回复前完成用户原话的保存和读回。正常最终回复发送结束后，在当轮自动保存实际发出的回复全文并读回，内容与实发文本逐字一致，无需用户再发消息。主记录可以独立写入，已发生的过程按 FR-004 和 FR-005 随本轮推进陆续写入。写入失败、恢复及不可控中断、Git 提交停写分别按 FR-006、FR-007、FR-008 处理。若采用 Hook，须在其通过首期会话记录验收后移除由其接替的旧记录逻辑。
205:- **FR-008** 记录入口维护跳过记录的场景，按实际操作步骤匹配，首个场景为 Git 提交。每次提交步骤开始至对应结果回复发出期间跳过记录写入，该时段由记录引入的工作区和暂存区新增差异为零。恢复时沿同一边界处理。用户原始请求、修复过程、两次提交间的修复和后续普通对话照常保存。
303:2026-11-26，当前工作区在真实提交 `ce0fab3` 后将结果回复写入主记录，同轮详情也保存了提交过程。FR-008 的停写要求在该现场失效，SC-004 在该场景未通过。用户将此项设为最高优先级 P0，由 T037 复现、修复并复验。 [用户原话](requirements/raw/2026-11-26-recording-commit-skip-regression.md) · [现场证据](analysis/recording-acceptance.md#2026-11-26-当前工作区提交停写回归) · [P0 任务](tasks.md#p0-提交停写回归)
333:同日，用户进一步明确目录选择和 Git 可见性。记录 Skill 已按 FR-003 在实际写入时判断目录，隔离 CLI 样例核对了三档选址和 Git 工作区可见性，已刷新当前工作区安装。FR-008 的提交边界及当前工作区的用户手动试用仍按更新后的 T033 判据复验。[本次确认](requirements/raw/2026-11-24-recording-directory-git-visibility.md) · [隔离用例与安装核对](implementation/T033.md#2026-11-24-写入时根据项目选择目录)
335:同日，用户在当前工作区新建 Codex CLI `0.172.0` 会话。首轮结束后已对照宿主事件核对首条原话、实际回复、索引定位和 Git 工作区可见性。T033 仍待用新版复验 FR-008 的提交及结果回复期间停写，并收集用户的手动验收结论。[当前工作区新会话证据](implementation/T033.md#2026-11-24-当前工作区新会话实测)
339:2026-11-20 确认关闭前未保存且原文丢失时保留缺档，`resume` 从已保存断点续写。提交场景按实际步骤跳过，验收见 FR-007、FR-008、SC-003 和 SC-004。 [用户确认原文](requirements/raw/2026-11-20-recording-exceptions.md)

在真实的「修复后提交」样例中，可回查用户请求、首次提交前的修复和后续普通对话。在同一轮提交失败、修复再重试的样例中，还可回查两次提交之间的修复。每次提交步骤及其结果回复期间的记录写入次数为零，且由记录引入的工作区和暂存区新增差异为零。普通轮次的主记录、各组详情和会话附件在 Git 工作区可见，符合提交约定的文件可随项目变更一同提交。\\n- **SC-005** 使用本仓库至少两个项目目录的已保存材料得到一份协作 Skill。该次生成的每条规则均可经提炼文档回查到原始依据，参与材料的来源均可核实位于本仓库目录内。首次自动提炼时，每条特点的依据均可回查新流程记录。两个父会话从同一版本派发分析后，先采纳的结果推进当前版本，随后返回的旧结果及与删除交错的旧结果均不回退当前规则、依据关联或目录内容。\\n- **SC-006** 一位使用者将产物目录带到另一项目后，兼容代理可以仅凭该目录在至少一个代表性任务中正确应用其中的规则。\\n- **SC-007** 删除测试中，一条规则及其独占提炼依据被移除，共享依据及原始记录完整可查。后续符合条件的一次处理能够基于保留的材料重新形成该规则。\\n- **SC-008** 旧技能的归档内容与原件一致，项目经正常安装或同步后可发现的旧入口数量为零。\\n- **SC-009** 采用通用 Skill 格式核验时，记录、提炼和分析三份 Skill 各有一个独立、可读的入口及可定位的必要资源，各自的输入、触发条件和产物都能核对。\\n- **SC-010** 在隔离用例中让父代理启动两个业务子代理但不等待，至少一个子代理在下一用户轮开始后才回复。用不同的已知输入和回复对照同一主记录的顺序、时间和代理标注；主代理回复在子代理结束前已保存并可回查，两份子代理回复按实际时序写入且不混淆身份。无子代理的对照保持原有样式和保存时机。\\n- **SC-011** 打开实际生成的普通对话主记录，核对同轮及跨轮的每两个相邻消息块之间均可看到分隔；多行消息、消息正文内的分隔符及代码块原样回读，详情链接仍可用。恢复续写后新边界不漏、不重复；主、子代理时间线以 SC-010 的交错样例补验同样的显示规则。\\n\\n## Assumptions\\n52:- 用户确认记录 Skill 安装即启用，并明确首期资料只落在项目目录内。用户按日常方式启动 Codex 即进入既有记录流程。会话范围、提交例外和写入失败依 FR-006～FR-008 处理。[用户原话](requirements/raw/2026-11-24-recording-install-enables.md)\\n197:- **FR-002** 项目级记录安装后，用户通过 Codex 原生流程对本次安装的记录 Hook 确认一次并启用记录。同一项目的 Hook 定义和 Codex 信任状态保持不变时，后续普通启动无需再次确认。用户在本仓库根目录或子目录按日常方式运行 `codex` 并发送消息，无需手改配置、点名记录 Skill 或另行启动记录。每个符合条件的交互轮次都自动进入记录流程，包括模型未选用记录 Skill 且零模型工具调用的轮次。用户发出的第一条正常对话消息触发 FR-003 建档，后续轮次沿同一会话续写。纳入记录的用户发言须在对应业务动作前保存并读回原文。无业务动作时，在开始发送最终回复前完成用户原话的保存和读回。正常最终回复发送结束后，在当轮自动保存实际发出的回复全文并读回，内容与实发文本逐字一致，无需用户再发消息。主记录可以独立写入，已发生的过程按 FR-004 和 FR-005 随本轮推进陆续写入。写入失败、恢复及不可控中断、Git 提交停写分别按 FR-006、FR-007、FR-008 处理。若采用 Hook，须在其通过首期会话记录验收后移除由其接替的旧记录逻辑。\\n205:- **FR-008** 记录入口维护跳过记录的场景，按实际操作步骤匹配，首个场景为 Git 提交。每次提交步骤开始至对应结果回复发出期间跳过记录写入，该时段由记录引入的工作区和暂存区新增差异为零。恢复时沿同一边界处理。用户原始请求、修复过程、两次提交间的修复和后续普通对话照常保存。\\n303:2026-11-26，当前工作区在真实提交 `ce0fab3` 后将结果回复写入主记录，同轮详情也保存了提交过程。FR-008 的停写要求在该现场失效，SC-004 在该场景未通过。用户将此项设为最高优先级 P0，由 T037 复现、修复并复验。 [用户原话](requirements/raw/2026-11-26-recording-commit-skip-regression.md) · [现场证据](analysis/recording-acceptance.md#2026-11-26-当前工作区提交停写回归) · [P0 任务](tasks.md#p0-提交停写回归)\\n333:同日，用户进一步明确目录选择和 Git 可见性。记录 Skill 已按 FR-003 在实际写入时判断目录，隔离 CLI 样例核对了三档选址和 Git 工作区可见性，已刷新当前工作区安装。FR-008 的提交边界及当前工作区的用户手动试用仍按更新后的 T033 判据复验。[本次确认](requirements/raw/2026-11-24-recording-directory-git-visibility.md) · [隔离用例与安装核对](implementation/T033.md#2026-11-24-写入时根据项目选择目录)\\n335:同日，用户在当前工作区新建 Codex CLI `0.172.0` ## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_68ba9bbb8d8440a7bad8c95e9416ef07`

- 来源定位：字节 947578

~~~text
工作目录：/home/by152/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const base=load("t037Base");const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`PYTHONPATH="$PWD/packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording" PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B - '${base}' <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import json, shlex, sys
from recording.events import collect, commands_from, git_actions, is_commit, commit_verification
base=Path(sys.argv[1])
for mode in ("direct", "code"):
    print(mode)
    p,= (base/(mode+"-repro")/"state/sessions").rglob("*.jsonl")
    data=p.read_bytes()
    offset=0
    turn=None
    for line in data.splitlines(keepends=True):
        row=json.loads(line)
        entry=row.get("payload") or {}
        if row["type"]=="event_msg" and entry.get("type")=="task_started":
            if turn: break
            turn=entry["turn_id"]
            start=offset
        if row["type"]=="response_item" and entry.get("type") in {"custom_tool_call","function_call"} and entry.get("call_id","").startswith("t035-"):
            value=entry.get("input",entry.get("arguments"))
            actions=[act for cmd in commands_from(value) for act in git_actions(cmd)]
            print("provider",entry["call_id"],actions, "commit",is_commit(value),"verify",commit_verification(value))
        item=entry.get("item") or {}
        if row["type"]=="event_msg" and entry.get("type")=="item_completed" and item.get("type")=="CommandExecution":
            argv=item["command"]
            parsed=list(git_actions(shlex.join(argv)))
            script=list(git_actions(argv[-1]))
            print("host",item.get("exit_code"),"argv length",len(argv),"lines",len(argv[-1].splitlines()),"parser on joined argv",parsed,"parser on shell script",script)
        offset += len(line)
    scope=SimpleNamespace(offset=start, transcript=p, turn=turn, cwd=base/(mode+"-repro")/"repo")
    result=collect(scope)
    print("commit_result at Stop",result.commit_result)
PY`,max_output_tokens:3000});text(r);

~~~

## 进度原话

- 来源定位：字节 967358

~~~text
隔离复现成功。Code Mode 会把失败和成功的提交过程写进详情，两条入口都会误记后续 Git 核对和最终回复。宿主原生事件保留了完整命令，我再做一组单行对照，确认多行命令是在哪一步丢失的。
~~~

const base=load("t037Base");const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`python3 - '${base}' <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import sys
base = Path(sys.argv[1])
p = base / "probe.py"
s = p.read_text()
s = s.replace('git add README.md\\\\ngit ', 'git add README.md records\\\\ngit ')
needle = '''                          "workspaceRecordStatusAfterReply": git_status, "indexRecordDiffAfterReply": index_diff},'''
s = s.replace(needle, '''                          "gitReportedBeforeStop": str(outputs["t035-check"]),
                          "trackedRecordDiffAfterReply": subprocess.check_output(
                              ["git", "diff", "--name-only", "HEAD", "--", "records"], cwd=repo, text=True).splitlines(),
                          "workspaceRecordStatusAfterReply": git_status, "indexRecordDiffAfterReply": index_diff},''')
needle = '''\\n\\n\\ndef formal_message'''
s = s.replace(needle, '''\\n\\nif os.environ.get("T037_SINGLE") == "1":
    COMMIT_STEPS = tuple((call, command.replace("\\\\n", " && ")) for call, command in COMMIT_STEPS)
\\n\\ndef formal_message''')
p.write_text(s)
for mode in ("direct-staged", "code-staged", "code-single"):
    work = base / mode
    (work / "state").mkdir(parents=True)
    (work / "scratch").mkdir()
PY
python3 -B - '${base}' <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import sys
p = Path(sys.argv[1]) / "probe.py"
compile(p.read_text(), str(p), "exec")
PY`});text(r)

## 工具结果 `exec-f2e09005-f028-4863-b4cc-e07a644335a4`

~~~text
---
name: verification-before-completion
description: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always
---

# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
❌ Trust agent report
```

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

~~~

## 工具调用 `exec` · `call_9aa88eccfeeb43fb83ac3c068f43785d`

- 来源定位：字节 1059404

~~~text
工作目录：/home/by152/workspace/ena/agent-foundry/feature/init
const base=load("t037Base");
const r=await tools.exec_command({cmd:`PYTHONPATH="$PWD/packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording" PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 python3 -B - '${base}' <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import json, shlex, subprocess, sys
from recording.events import collect, commands_from, git_actions
base = Path(sys.argv[1])
features = Path("specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill")
destination = features / "analysis/acceptance/t037-commit-skip-reproduction.json"
reports = []
for variant in ("direct-staged", "code-staged", "code-single"):
    work = base / variant
    repo = work / "repo"
    result = json.loads((work / "result.json").read_text())
    p, = (work / "state/sessions").rglob("*.jsonl")
    offset = 0
    turn = None
    provider = []
    commands = []
    for line in p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True):
        row = json.loads(line)
        entry = row.get("payload") or {}
        if row["type"] == "event_msg" and entry.get("type") == "task_started":
            if turn:
                break
            turn = entry["turn_id"]
            start = offset
        if row["type"] == "response_item" and entry.get("type") in {"custom_tool_call", "function_call"} and entry.get("call_id", "").startswith("t035-"):
            value = entry.get("input", entry.get("arguments"))
            provider.append({
                "tool": entry["type"],
                "commandActionsFromToolInput": [action for cmd in commands_from(value) for action in git_actions(cmd)],
            })
        item = entry.get("item") or {}
        if row["type"] == "event_msg" and entry.get("type") == "item_completed" and item.get("type") == "CommandExecution":
            argv = item["command"]
            commands.append({
                "shellScript": argv[-1],
                "exitCode": item["exit_code"],
                "actionsFromNativeArgvRejoinedForParser": list(git_actions(shlex.join(argv))),
                "actionsFromNativeShellScriptArgument": list(git_actions(argv[-1])),
            })
        offset += len(line)
    assert len(provider) == len(commands) == 5
    scope = SimpleNamespace(offset=start, transcript=p, turn=turn, cwd=repo)
    assert commands[1]["exitCode"] == 128 and commands[3]["exitCode"] == 0
    read_only_clean = "records/20" not in result.pop("gitReportedBeforeStop")
    assert read_only_clean
    result.pop("trackedRecordDiffAfterReply")  # 此读数采自后续普通轮次，提交回复的差异以此前的状态为准。
    workspace_status = result.pop("workspaceRecordStatusAfterReply")
    index_status = result.pop("indexRecordDiffAfterReply")
    detail, = list(repo.glob(f"records/*/details/*/{turn}.md"))
    committed = subprocess.check_output(
        ["git", "show", "HEAD:" + detail.relative_to(repo).as_posix()], cwd=repo, text=True)
    assert result["commitReplySaved"] == (variant != "code-single")
    assert result["authorFailureInDetail"] == (variant == "code-staged")
    assert result["successCommandInDetail"] == (variant == "code-staged")
    assert len(workspace_status) == (0 if variant == "code-single" else 2)
    assert not index_status
    result.update({
        "variant": variant,
        "gitRecordedNoRecordChangesAtStartOfReadonlyCheck": read_only_clean,
        "workspaceChangedRecordFilesAfterReply": ["detail" if "/details/" in line else "main" for line in workspace_status],
        "indexChangedRecordCountAfterReply": len(index_status),
        "failureStagedIntoRetryCommit": "Author identity unknown" in committed,
        "commitResultFlagWhenReplayingStop": collect(scope).commit_result,
        "nativeToolSteps": [{**a, **b} for a, b in zip(provider, commands, strict=True)],
    })
    reports.append(result)
data = {
    "task": "T037",
    "baseline": "1233ff9",
    "date": "2026-11-26",
    "environment": "Linux，独立 Codex 状态，真实交互式终端与 Git，回环端点返回合成工具请求与回复",
    "source": "使用当前工作区安装副本内的安装器重新本地安装，核对除安装归属清单外全量文件哈希一致",
    "ordinaryReplyAfterCommitWasTested": True,
    "resumeAfterCommitWasTested": False,
    "variants": reports,
}
destination.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\\n")
print(destination)
PY`,max_output_tokens:1600});text(r);

~~~

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: specs/skills/002-conversation-to-skill/analysis/recording-acceptance.md\n@@\n 现场结果违反 FR-008，SC-004 在该场景未通过。依原验收口径，每次提交开始到对应结果回复发出期间，主记录、详情和附件的写入次数为零，由记录引入的工作区和暂存区新增差异为零。用户原始请求、提交前和两次提交之间的修复及后续普通对话均可回查。T037 以实际安装副本和工具事件顺序复现，再用正式来源验证上述边界。 [需求与验收](../spec.md#当前实施状态) · [T037](../tasks.md#p0-提交停写回归)\n+\n+### T037 隔离复现\n+\n+从当前工作区的记录 Skill 安装副本复制安装，在独立的 Codex CLI `0.172.0` 终端信任三个项目 Hook。用回环端点发送合成工具请求，通过宿主执行真实 Git 命令。同轮先修改 README，再故意以缺少作者名的配置提交，修改 README 后补足作者名重试。成功后执行 `git status` 和 `git log`，等待宿主发出最终回复，再核对记录文件的哈希、修改时间和 Git 状态。提交请求和两次提交之间的修复可回查，下一普通轮次的回复也正常保存。 [逐步观测](acceptance/t037-commit-skip-reproduction.json)\n+\n+| 工具入口 | 详情误记 | 提交结果回复误入主记录 | 回复后由记录引入的 Git 差异 |\n+| --- | --- | --- | --- |\n+| Code Mode，多行命令 | 失败、成功的提交与只读核对。失败输出在后续修复时被补写并进入重试提交 | 有 | 工作区两个文件，暂存区零 |\n+| 直接命令入口，多行命令 | 只读核对 | 有 | 工作区两个文件，暂存区零 |\n+| Code Mode，同一组命令改用单行 `&&` | 无 | 无 | 工作区及暂存区均为零 |\n+\n+宿主完成事件的 `command` 参数数组保留了完整脚本。`completed_item()` 将数组拼成带引号的命令，再交给 `command_segments()` 按行切开，导致 shell 脚本的引号在解析前被截断。实际事件中，提交脚本只识别出 `add`，只读脚本也丢失了 `status` 和 `log`。Code Mode 通过 JavaScript 变量拼装的脚本还无法从工具输入识别提交。重放已捕获事件时，多行样例在 `Stop` 的停写判断为假，单行对照为真。 [事件解析](../../../../packages/skills/src/profile/conversation-recording/recording/events.py) · [逐步观测](acceptance/t037-commit-skip-reproduction.json)\n+\n+本次保存修复前基线。现有 Python 记录回归 17 项通过。修复后正式安装复验及恢复续写场景待执行。\n*** End Patch"));

assert content["variants"][1]["failureStagedIntoRetryCommit"]
assert all(row["realAuthorIdentityFailure"] and row["realRetrySuccess"] and row["laterOrdinaryReplySaved"] for row in content["variants"])
PY`];const rs=await Promise.allSettled(cmds.map(cmd=>tools.exec_command({cmd,max_output_tokens:3100})));for(const r of rs)text(r.value??r.reason);
